Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the oscillations framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 170. The resulting operator achieves bound O(906/n^4).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the oscillations decomposition. By minimality, the element a^170 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 906-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 222.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: For any oscillations module M satisfying the optimality condition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 73. The projective resolution has length at most 69 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 73 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the oscillations structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 69. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
